Daily Water Intelligence For South Korea

K-WaterGuard AI Agent Overview

K-WaterGuard AI is an autonomous water-quality intelligence dashboard for South Korea. It combines Korean monitoring data, historical station records, spatial maps, alert screening rules, trend analysis, and researcher-ready downloads into one decision-support system.

The agent supports farmers, watershed managers, lake and reservoir operators, researchers, extension officers, policy experts, and environmental agencies by turning raw water-quality records into practical signals for irrigation safety, nutrient pressure, algal-bloom risk, pollution screening, and long-term watershed planning.

  • Farmers can review water-quality status before irrigation planning and understand nutrient or algal risk around nearby watersheds.
  • Researchers can download historical station datasets and inspect Mann-Kendall and Sen's slope trend outputs.
  • Water managers can track alerts, spatial hotspots, watershed status, and station coverage with consistent Korean units and standards.
Open Data Page Open Trend Page Open Algal Bloom Page

Research Lab And Creator

Created By

This dashboard is created for the Regional Water Environment System Lab, Department of Agricultural Engineering, Gyeongsang National University.

The lab works on water resource conservation, watershed management, agricultural water quality, AI, big-data analytics, remote sensing, and hydrological modeling for sustainable agriculture and evidence-based water policy.
